- **`NodeForm` resolves and populates the value.** `useResolvedTemplate` (previously used only by the custom-field renderer, now exported from it) is applied to the built-in name/description/slug inputs: the template resolves against the node's field data and the derived value is written into the read-only input, so it both displays and submits. It recomputes as the source fields change.
- **The server accepts the client-computed value.** `updateNode`'s built-in lock no longer treats `computedTemplate` as locked — the same client-computed trust model as custom computed fields (`createNode` already accepted it). `system` / `admin` `editableBy` locks are still enforced server-side. Net: a computed built-in becomes API-writable, exactly like a custom computed field — no privilege change (it is the caller's own node).

<Aside type="note">
Generated **frontend + backend** change — validate with a host `tsc --noEmit -p` after install, per the module's [validation discipline](/module-starter/building-modules/).
</Aside>

## v1.23.2

**Embeddings heal: detect the datasource pgvector declaration, advise only when missing**

The `enableEmbeddings` heal previously printed a "declare pgvector in your datasource" recommendation on *every* successful run — including apps that already had it — and framed it as a manual edit, which doesn't fit a generated app: the `generator`/`datasource` blocks are **starter-template-owned** (modules contribute models only).

- **Conditional + actionable advisory.** The heal now reads the host schema (`schema.prisma`, or `schema/base.prisma` in multi-file layouts) and only warns when the pgvector extension isn't declared — silent once it is. When it warns, it points at the **starter's** enable path (`scripts/enable-vectors.ps1`, or the "Enable vector database?" prompt on `update-template`) instead of implying a hand-edit.
- **Why the starter, not structure:** the datasource declaration is one per database and shared by every vector-using module; structure only *detects* it. A planned starter primitive (a non-interactive `ensure-pgvector`) will later let the heal flip from *advise* to *auto-enable*.

Module-only change — no template or generated-output delta from v1.23.1, so no host re-validation required.

## v1.23.1

**`reconfigure` heals embeddings — the same-version opt-in now provisions pgvector**

Structure now consumes the module-starter's **`reconfigure`** command (scaffold v1.24.0): `npx structure reconfigure --set enableEmbeddings=true` re-renders the schema and embeddings-gated files and re-runs the module migrations at the *same* module version. But a same-version reconfigure deliberately does **not** fire `onUpdate` (a version-transition heal), so on its own it left a gap — a fresh opt-in flip would not run the pgvector extension/index provisioning or the P3009 failed-migration recovery.

- **New `onReconfigure` lifecycle hook.** When `reconfigure` flips `enableEmbeddings` **off → on**, the hook runs the same `healEmbeddingsSetup` as a fresh install: extension setup, failed-migration resolve + `migrate deploy`, and HNSW index creation. It is gated on the transition, so a reconfigure that only changes `embeddingDimensions` (still a manual migration by design) or that leaves embeddings off does nothing.
- **Runtime `ensureInfrastructure()` remains the backstop** for anything that bypasses the hook (e.g. a hand-edited `.module-state.json`).

Scaffold-and-hook change only — no template or generated-output delta from v1.23.0, so no host re-validation is required. See [Cognitive Foundations → Opting in later](/modules/structure/cognitive/#opting-in-later).

## v1.23.0

**Trust-fix release — every admin control is now real (or gone)**

A pass over "declared but dead" controls: settings the admin UI shipped, persisted, and *promised* but which nothing read. Each was a trust/correctness gap, not just a missing feature.

- **`builtInFieldConfig` editableBy is enforced.** The Name / Description / Slug `editableBy` setting (`anyone` / `admin` / `system`, plus `computedTemplate`) is now read by `NodeForm` (the inputs render read-only when locked) **and** re-checked server-side in `updateNode` — a direct API write to a locked built-in field is ignored, not silently applied. Custom-field `editableBy` is also applied inside the `NodeForm` create/edit loop (it was previously only enforced on the detail page). *(The `computedTemplate` **auto-compute** for built-in fields — writing the derived value — remains a follow-up; a `computedTemplate` field is treated as read-only for now.)*
- **`NodeChildrenTab` create buttons respect `permissions.create`.** They previously rendered from `allowedChildren` alone; a child type the user cannot create is no longer offered.
- **Server-side sort by custom (data) fields.** `dataSortField` / `dataSortType` were accepted and validated by the API but ignored by the service, and the table sorted only the current page — a page-local lie. `listNodes` now sorts the full matching set by the JSON field (type-aware: number / date / text, empties last, stable tiebreak) and returns the correct page; `NodeTable` data-field columns drive this server sort, while module-registered (computed) columns still sort client-side.
- **Conditional-colour rules show option labels.** Rule rows are now a Select of the source field's options (label shown, value stored) and warn when a saved rule value no longer matches any option.
- **Chained-select admin editor.** The `chained-select` field type could only be configured via JSON import; it now has an admin editor for its levels and (two-level) options tree.

**Dropped (dead contract, no consumers):**

- **`renderCompact`** (`NodeColumnRegistration`) — no view ever read it; removed from the contract and the docs.
- **`inputMask`** (`NodeTypeField`) — never wired to a renderer and overlapping `validation.pattern` / `phoneConfig`; the orphaned declaration is removed.

<Aside type="note">
Shipped as a **minor** despite the two contract removals — both were dead (zero consumers), so removing them changes no working behaviour. Generated frontend code should be validated with a host `tsc --noEmit -p` after install, per the module's [validation discipline](/module-starter/building-modules/).
</Aside>

## v1.22.0

**Chunk storage — a dedicated `structure_chunk` table with vector search**

Node embeddings (v1.16.0) cover *whole-record* similarity; retrieval-augmented workflows also need **sub-node spans** — the chunks a node's content is split into, each embedded on its own. This release adds a dedicated **`structure_chunk`** table and a chunk-scoped write/search surface, mirroring the node-embedding foundation. Structure owns chunk storage and search; the engine owns chunking and generation.

- **Opt-in on the same flag.** Chunk storage rides the existing `enableEmbeddings` prompt (no new question) and the same `embeddingDimensions`. On embeddings-enabled installs it adds the `structure_chunk` model, a partial HNSW index (module migration `1.22.0__chunk-storage.sql`), and a chunk erasure handler; on other installs, none of it is generated.
- **New methods on `structureEmbeddings`:** `replaceNodeChunks` (atomic re-chunk), `setChunkEmbedding`, `listChunksNeedingEmbedding`, `getNodeChunks`, `deleteNodeChunks`, and `semanticSearchChunks`. The last **joins `structure_nodes`**, so the source node's status (default `['active']`) and optional `nodeTypes` filter apply — a chunk of an archived or deleted node never surfaces.
- **Two new action hooks:** `structure.node.chunksReplaced` (`{ userId, nodeId, count }`) and `structure.chunk.embeddingUpdated` (`{ userId, chunkId, embeddingModel }`).
- **Capability probe** now reports `chunks: { enabled }` alongside `embeddings` (it mirrors `embeddings.enabled`).
- **Lifecycle:** `nodeId` is a plain FK with no DB cascade — a node's soft-delete is handled by the search join, re-chunking is delete + insert, and chunks are hard-removed only at account erasure. The runtime `ensureInfrastructure()` and install heal both provision the chunk HNSW index. See [Cognitive Foundations → Chunk storage](/modules/structure/cognitive/#chunk-storage).

## v1.21.1

**`NodeTypeFieldType` accepts module-registered type ids**

The field-type union was closed to the 21 built-in literals, so a module-registered field type id — valid at runtime through `nodeFieldTypeRegistry` — had no type-level acknowledgement. Widened with `(string & {})`, which keeps editor autocomplete for the built-in literals while accepting any string, so a module's custom type id type-checks cleanly on `NodeTypeField.type`.

## v1.21.0

**Field types are genuinely module-owned; `media` is no longer built in**

<Aside type="caution" title="Breaking: the built-in `media` field type is removed">
Structure no longer ships a `media` field type. A storage module (e.g. document-management) registers one through `nodeFieldTypeRegistry`; where no such module is installed, `media` is not offered.

Removed: the `media` entry in the type union and built-in registrations, both renderer arms, `mediaConfig`, the `MediaValue`/`MediaUploadHandler` types, and the `nodeMediaUploadRegistry` introduced in v1.20.0 — a full field-type registration covers upload UI, config and display in **one** mechanism instead of two.

A node type that still references `type: "media"` with no provider installed renders **read-only with an explanation and its value preserved**. Shipped as a minor rather than a major because nothing is in production yet; there is deliberately no deprecation shim.
</Aside>

Field types were always registry-backed — Structure registers its own built-ins into `nodeFieldTypeRegistry` under `moduleId: 'structure'`, and the renderer already deferred to a module-registered type on both the edit and display paths, with a module type overriding a built-in of the same id. Three gaps stopped modules from actually owning a type; all three are now closed:

- **The admin field-type picker is registry-driven.** It was a hardcoded list, so a registered type could never be selected. It now derives from the registry (reactive), sorted by category then order, labelling non-Structure types with their providing module. A type appears when its module is installed and disappears when it is uninstalled.
- **`configSchema` renders.** A registered type's declared options appear in the admin field editor and are stored verbatim on the new `NodeTypeField.config`. Structure never interprets that object — the registering module reads it in its own renderer.
- **Unknown field types are safe.** Previously an unrecognised type fell through to a text input bound to `onChange(e.target.value)` — which would overwrite a stored array or object with a string. That is exactly what happens to a field left behind when its providing module is uninstalled, so it had to be fixed before field types could sensibly be module-owned. Such fields now render read-only with an explanation, preserving the value.

Also corrects a long-standing docs error: the `nodeFieldTypeRegistry` example used `category: 'content'`, which is not a valid category (`text | media | reference | layout | custom`).

## v1.20.0

**Roadmap verification — table view, three field renderers, server-side filtering, five bug fixes**

Every open roadmap item was re-verified against the code, which found the roadmap inaccurate in *both* directions and surfaced five defects nobody had logged. This release clears most of them.

**New: table view** — the column registry had been shipped and marked complete, but no table view existed: `useNodeColumns()` had zero call sites and admin-configured columns were written and never read. `NodeTable` merges admin-configured field columns with module-registered ones and honours the full registration contract (`render`, `width`, `minWidth`, `alignment`, `sortable`, `getSortValue`). Click-to-sort with asc/desc indicators; horizontal scroll on narrow viewports so no configured column disappears. `table` is restored to the admin and user-settings **Default view** selects, and declared in `shadcnComponents`.

**Field renderers implemented** — `media`, `multiselect` and `chained-select` previously fell through to a text input bound to `onChange(e.target.value)`, so editing one could **overwrite an array or object value with a string**:

- `multiselect` — toggle-button multi-pick stored as `string[]`
- `chained-select` — cascading selects from `chainedSelectConfig`; picking a level clears deeper ones
- `media` — file picker honouring `accept`/`maxSize`/`multiple`. Structure owns the field, not the storage, so a storage module contributes an uploader via the new **`nodeMediaUploadRegistry`**; with none registered the field degrades to http(s) URL entry rather than doing nothing
- `textarea` `size` now maps to rows (2/4/8) instead of a hardcoded 3, and `validation.pattern`/length are applied to text and textarea

**Server-side field filtering** — the list page mapped its field filters into a client-side `useMemo` over the fetched page, which was wrong past the first page: matches beyond the boundary were invisible and the count reflected the unfiltered set. It now maps them into `query.dataFilters`, using the filter engine that already existed.

**Bug fixes**

- `conditionalColor` was **write-only** — configured rules were never applied. New `resolveNodeColor()` applies them across `NodeIcon`, `NodeCard` and `NodeDetailHeader`
- The **"View Public" button dead-ended** into the protected catch-all (`/p/:publishedSlug` is not a route the module ships). Removed until public views land
- `StructureTree` chevrons **never worked uncontrolled** — `expandedIds` defaulted to a fresh empty `Set` with no internal state. Now self-managing unless both control props are supplied
- `getTree` was **unbounded**; capped at 5,000 rows (depth-ascending order means truncation can never orphan a child)

**Cross-module DX** — `useNodeName(id)`, `useNodeAncestors(id)`, an exported `StructureService` type, `structureService` re-exported from the barrel, and `getNodeFieldValue()` / `resolveNodeColor()` / `getEffectiveFields()` utilities. Toasts now name the node type ("Client created") instead of saying "Node".

<Aside type="note">
`useNodesByType(type)` and the frontend `structureService.createNode()` were listed as planned but had in fact already shipped — the roadmap simply never recorded them. Likewise `useNodeAncestors` existed as `useNodePath` (full root-to-self chain); the new hook returns strict ancestors.
</Aside>

## v1.19.2

**Generated code type-checks clean on Prisma 7.8 / React 19 hosts**

Patch release clearing 74 latent type errors (13 backend, 61 frontend) that had accumulated in the generated code. None were visible to the module's own build — it only type-checks the template *wrapper* strings, never the code inside them — so they surfaced only when a host app ran `tsc`. Verified by iterative host type-check: a fresh install is now **0 errors** on both projects.

Several were genuine host-contract mistakes rather than cast noise, and are worth knowing if you have local overrides:

- **`useSession()` returns `{ data }`** — the templates destructured a non-existent `session` property.
- **`PageHeader` has no `action` prop** — the list-page create button now sits beside the header instead of being passed into it.
- **`Dropdown` (settings-blocks) takes `onChange`**, not `onValueChange`.
- **`createModuleQueryKeys().key()` accepts only `string | number` segments** — the node list query is now serialised into the key, and the connection-types boolean maps to `'all'` / `'enabled'`.
- **`NavigationRegistry` has no `unregisterSection`** — the teardown call is now a capability probe.
- **Registration `nodeTypes` is optional** — the admin `getItemsForType` constraint required it, which broke item inference across the whole Integrations tab; loosening it (`undefined` ≡ `'*'`) fixed nine downstream errors at once.
- **`CardExtensionPosition` was declared twice** — now single-sourced in `node-type.types` and re-exported, clearing the barrel ambiguity (TS2308).
- Prisma 7's stricter `JsonValue` requires `as unknown as` bridges on Json-column reads; the same applies to the `lucide-react` namespace cast.

## v1.19.1

**Fix: `ErrorAlert` takes a string, not the `Error` object**

The admin node-types page passed the TanStack Query `error` object to `<ErrorAlert error={…}>`, whose prop is `string | null | undefined`. Caught by host type-check during v1.19.0 validation. Now passes `error.message`.

## v1.19.0

**Meta-audit remediation — nine criticals, 53 moderates, and the minor sweep**

A four-dimension audit (standards / production readiness / performance / security) with adversarial verification produced 171 confirmed findings; this release fixes all of the critical and moderate ones plus most minors. Highlights, grouped by what changes for you:

**Security**

- **Cross-user connection read closed (IDOR).** `createNode` / `bulkCreateNodes` now verify that client-supplied `fromNodeId` / `toNodeId` resolve to caller-owned nodes, `duplicateNode` verifies `targetParentId`, `children` includes carry a `userId` predicate, and connection endpoint hydration is owner-filtered. Previously a connection pointed at another user's node id returned that user's full node row.
- **`secret` fields are encrypted at rest** — AES-256-GCM via the starter security feature, stored as `{ __sealed: … }`, decrypted only on the owner's detail read. Existing plaintext values keep working and seal on their next write. See [Fields → secret](/modules/structure/fields/#secret).
- **Field-level `editableBy` is enforced server-side** — `PATCH /nodes/:id` now rejects writes to `system` fields (everyone) and `admin` fields (non-admins) with a 400. Public API-key callers are never admins for this check. Previously browser-only.
- **API keys can be given an expiry** from the create dialog (30 / 90 / 365 days or never; default 90). Expiry was already enforced server-side — the UI simply couldn't set it, so in-app keys were perpetual.
- `url` fields only render as links for `http(s)` values, so a stored `javascript:` URI can't become a clickable href.

**Correctness / data integrity**

- **Deletion handlers refuse to run unscoped.** Each account-erasure handler now throws if the subject carries no `userId`/`tenantId` — Prisma treats an `undefined` where-value as *omit the filter*, which would have turned the cascade into a whole-table delete.
- **`NodeForm` resets on open.** The single `:nodeType` route never remounts the form, so navigating `/clients` → `/projects` previously submitted the stale `nodeType` — saving a Project as a Client.
- **Bulk create is honestly partial-success** (see [Configuration](/modules/structure/configuration/)) instead of reporting phantom successes from an aborted transaction.
- Node-type `labels` [persist end-to-end](/modules/structure/usage/#labels-utility); `createNode` no longer drops the `settings` field its own schema accepts; `getNodeType` returns the same config fields as `listNodeTypes`.

**Dead wiring reconnected**

- Module-registered fields (via `structureRegistry.register({ fields })`) now actually render, with the Integrations tab controlling enable/label/order.
- Frontend lifecycle hooks fire: the delete veto (`onBeforeNodeDelete`) is honoured, and `onNodeCreate` / `onNodeDelete` / `onNodeMove` are notified.
- Code-registered node types survive database syncs instead of being wiped, and `register()` works after the first sync.
- The Structure settings page's **Default view** preference is now read by the list page. Three other toggles were **removed** — they configured sidebar features that don't exist yet, so they saved values nothing read.

**Frontend robustness**

- `onError` handlers on the connection-type, API-key and template mutations — a failed key revoke previously read as success.
- Fixed a hard render crash when an admin-defined select option had an empty value; added the missing loading state that flashed a false 404, and an error state that previously read as "No types yet"; the config import can no longer double-submit and now reports per-item failures.

**Install blockers** — generated code now compiles on React 19 strict hosts: zero-argument `useRef` ×3, a non-narrowing `.filter(Boolean)`, a `listConnectionTypes` argument mismatch, `typeof formData.fields[0]` on an optional array, and **`@dnd-kit/{core,sortable,utilities}` were imported but never declared** in `frontend.dependencies` — a fresh install didn't build. (Longer term the starter will ship these as base dependencies; see the [starter roadmap](/starter-template/roadmap/).)

**Performance** — `updateDescendantPaths` is one statement instead of one UPDATE per descendant; `duplicateNode` derives descendant paths in memory instead of a SELECT each; `getNextSortOrder` accepts a transaction client instead of escaping the enclosing transaction; `listNodes` runs its count and page fetch concurrently; `getNodeConnections` is bounded; the API-key `lastUsedAt` write is throttled; `reorderNodes` is capped at 500 ids.

<Aside type="note">
`labels` adds a column to `structure_node_type`. Run `npx structure update` then `npx prisma migrate dev` in your backend to pick it up.
</Aside>

## v1.18.0

**Type Hierarchy — is-a inheritance for node types**

Node types can now declare parent types, forming a multiple-inheritance *is-a* DAG: a `court-order` is-a `legal-document` and is-a `event`; both are is-a `entity`. Subtypes inherit **fields and features** from their ancestors (nearest-first), and connection allowlists become subsumption-aware. Full guide: [Type Hierarchy](/modules/structure/type-hierarchy/).

- **`superClasses` column on `structure_node_type`** — a `Json @default("[]")` array of parent type slugs. Purely additive; the host app picks it up with a normal `prisma migrate dev` after `npx structure update`. Validated on every write path (create/update/import/propose/approve): parents must be live types, no self-inheritance, no cycles, no redundant parents, max 10 parents, duplicates deduped.
- **Resolved config on `/types` responses** — list and get now carry `superClasses` (raw) and a read-only `resolved = { fields, features, ancestors }`: own fields first, then inherited fields in linearisation order (shadowed by field id, tagged `inheritedFrom`); features merge per-key nearest-wins, and an own key *always* wins — so `cognitiveIngest: { enabled: false }` on a subtype opts out of an inherited enable. Write paths strip `resolved` and any inherited field entries. Only fields and features inherit — rules, workflow, context hints, navigation, templates, permissions and the rest deliberately don't (v1 scope).
- **Subsumption-aware connection allowlists** — `createConnection` `fromTypes`/`toTypes` now admit subtypes of listed types: a "cites" connection type allowing `legal-document` accepts a `court-order` node. Empty list still means any type.
- **`'*'` wildcard fix** — a literal `'*'` entry in `fromTypes`/`toTypes` now means "any type"; previously it was documented but matched nothing, blocking every connection that used it.
- **`includeSubtypes` on node listing** — `listNodes` gains an `includeSubtypes` option (REST query param `?includeSubtypes=true`, service option, frontend client) that expands the type filter to self + all transitive subtypes.
- **Four new service methods** — `getTypeAncestors`, `getTypeDescendants`, `isSubtypeOf`, `expandTypeSlugs` via `serviceRegistry.get('structure')`; see [Integration](/modules/structure/integration/).
- **Admin UI** — the Hierarchy tab gains a **Type Inheritance (is-a)** picker (multiple parents, cycle-guarded); the Fields tab shows inherited fields read-only with their source type. V1 limitation: inherited fields aren't selectable in card/table/filter configuration pickers.
- **Import/export/proposals** — export includes `superClasses`; import validates the final set transactionally (unknown parents, cycles, or orphaned survivors roll the whole import back — note imports use whole-row semantics, so importing a pre-v1.18 export resets `superClasses` to `[]`). `proposeNodeType` accepts `superClasses`, validated at propose time and re-validated at approval.
- **Delete guard** — `deleteNodeType` is blocked while any live type lists the slug in its `superClasses` (the error names the referencing types).
- **Hierarchy-aware embedding selection** — `listNodesNeedingEmbedding`'s default type selection now includes subtypes of `cognitiveIngest`-enabled types without restating the flag. The `publicApi` feature is deliberately *not* hierarchy-aware — a subtype of a publicly-exposed type is not auto-exposed (security decision).

<Aside type="caution">
**Behaviour changes:** (1) a literal `'*'` in `fromTypes`/`toTypes` now means **any type** — previously it matched nothing and blocked every connection using it. (2) Connection allowlists **admit subtypes** once `superClasses` are configured — a type listed in `fromTypes`/`toTypes` now also accepts its subtypes. (3) `/types` responses gained `superClasses` and `resolved` keys — additive, but strict response validators should expect them.
</Aside>

---

## v1.17.0

**`contextHints` / `contextConfig` now persist**

Both fields have existed on `NodeTypeConfig`, the zod schemas, and the export format since v1.10.0 — but the `structure_node_type` table had no columns for them, so every write path silently dropped them and `getNodeContext` always saw them as unset.

- **New columns on `structure_node_type`** — `contextHints` (`String? @db.Text`) and `contextConfig` (`Json @default("{}")`). Purely additive; the host app picks them up with a normal `prisma migrate dev` after `npx structure update`.
- **All write paths now include them** — `createNodeType`, `updateNodeType`, `importNodeTypes`, and `proposeNodeType` persist both fields, so JSON imports and module-proposed node types (e.g. cognitive-pipeline's working-memory type) can ship context hints.
- **Reads are direct column reads** — the unchecked `(type as Record<string, unknown>)` casts in `listNodeTypes`/`getNodeType` are gone; `GET /nodes/:id/context` now actually honours `contextHints` and the `contextConfig` assembly toggles.
- **Proposals surface them** — `NodeTypeProposal` includes `contextHints`/`contextConfig`, so admins reviewing a proposal can see the proposed context configuration.

---

## v1.16.0

**Cognitive foundations (cognitive-db v2 RFC Phase 1)**

Additive groundwork for cognitive processing on top of Structure — vector embeddings, discovered-connection provenance, and module-proposed node types. Full reference: [Cognitive Foundations](/modules/structure/cognitive/).

- **Opt-in pgvector embeddings** — a new `enableEmbeddings` install prompt (default off; needs pgvector-capable Postgres, e.g. Neon) adds `embedding vector(n)` / `embeddingText` / `embeddingModel` columns to `structure_node` plus a partial HNSW cosine index. Dimensions (1024/512/1536/3072) are chosen at install.
- **`structureEmbeddings` service** — a new backend service (registered in the `serviceRegistry`, generated only when embeddings are enabled): `setNodeEmbedding`, `clearNodeEmbedding`, `listNodesNeedingEmbedding`, `semanticSearchNodes`, `findSimilarNodes`, and a memoised `ensureInfrastructure()`. In-process service API only — no HTTP routes. `structureService.getCognitiveCapabilities()` probes availability.
- **Install heal** — `onInstall`/`onUpdate` repair the first-install ordering gap (the `vector` extension must exist before Prisma can add the column): they run the setup SQL via `prisma db execute`, roll back and re-apply a failed migration, and re-ensure the index. All idempotent.
- **Connection origin/status** — `createConnection` accepts `origin` (a provenance tag stamped into `data.origin`; `'explicit'` default, well-known values `'discovered'` and `'active'`) and `status` (`'active'` default, or `'pending'`). Pending connections fire `structure.connection.proposed` and go live via `POST /connections/:id/promote`, which re-validates uniqueness/limits and endpoint liveness. New `GET /connections` listing with status/origin/type filters and pagination.
- **Node-type proposal/approval API** — modules propose node types (`POST /types/proposals` — pending, disabled, invisible to every other node-type query); admins list (`GET /types/proposals`), approve (`POST /types/proposals/:slug/approve`, user or system scope), or reject (`POST /types/proposals/:slug/reject`, which deletes the row and frees the slug). Approval takes the type live and fires `structure.nodeType.created`.
- **`features.cognitiveIngest`** — per-node-type feature flag (the "Cognitive Processing" toggle on the Properties tab) marking which types participate in cognitive processing; read by `listNodesNeedingEmbedding` and, in later phases, the cognitive-pipeline module.
- **Six new action hooks** — `structure.connection.proposed` / `.promoted`, `structure.node.embeddingUpdated`, and `structure.nodeType.proposed` / `.proposalApproved` / `.proposalRejected`. `structure.connection.created` and `structure.nodeType.created` now mean "went live" — they fire at creation-as-active *or* at promotion/approval.
- **First module migration** — `1.16.0__cognitive-foundations.sql` ships via the scaffold's per-module migration runner (idempotent, exception-safe `DO` blocks that degrade to notices on missing privileges). Because the module now carries a DB migration, **`npx structure init` and `npx structure update` need a reachable database** via `backend/.env` (`DIRECT_DATABASE_URL`/`DATABASE_URL`).

<Aside type="caution">
**Behaviour changes:** `getNodeConnections` now returns **active connections only** by default — archived/deleted connections were previously included and are now hidden unless you pass a wider `status` filter (`getNodeContext` inherits the new default). To match, `restoreNode` now restores the connections its own delete cascade soft-deleted. Also, **`origin` is now a reserved connection field id** — connection types can no longer define a custom field with id `origin` (the provenance stamp would overwrite it).
</Aside>

---

## v1.15.1

**Navigation auto-prime no longer retries on rate-limit (429)**

The module-scope navigation auto-prime (`navigation.registration.ts`) retried on *any* error with backoff. That backoff exists for the JWT-not-landed-yet race (a `401`), but retrying on a `429` (rate limited) spends more of the request budget the server just asked us to back off from — amplifying a rate-limit storm. The retry is now scoped to `401`/`403` only; `429`, `5xx`, and network errors give up silently (a later `useNodeTypes()` repopulates the registry).

<Aside type="tip">
The hook queries (`useNodeTypes`, etc.) still follow TanStack Query's default retry. To stop *all* queries app-wide from retrying a `429`, set a retry predicate on your `QueryClient` (e.g. don't retry when `error.response.status === 429`) — that's an app-level concern, not per-module.
</Aside>

---

## v1.15.0

**Public, API-key-authed REST API — per node type**

A new external REST surface exposes node *instances* of opt-in node types to integrations, authenticated with API keys instead of the in-app session. The internal session-scoped API (`/api/v1/structure/*`) is unchanged.

- **Opt-in per type** — a node type is reachable only when its new **Public REST API** feature toggle is on; unknown/non-exposed types return `404` (no existence leak). External base: `/api/v1/public/structure/:type`.
- **Methods** — `GET` (list + one), `POST`, `PATCH`. There is intentionally **no `DELETE`**. Handlers are a thin layer over the existing `structureService`, so all field/constraint validation and per-account scoping are reused.
- **API keys** — a new **API Keys** admin tab creates keys (the raw key is shown **once**), lists them, and revokes them. Keys carry a scope (`read`, or `read` + `write`); a write with a read-only key returns `403`. Keys are stored only as a `hashSecret()` hash via the security feature, declared as a `hashedSecret`, cascaded on account deletion, and resolved by an API-key middleware (`Authorization: Bearer` / `X-API-Key`).
- New table `structure_api_keys`; new docs page [Public REST API](/modules/structure/public-api/).

<Aside type="note">
The public router registers with `requiresAuth: false` so the starter mounts it without session auth. As with all template changes, the definitive check is `npx structure update` + a build/run in a starter app.
</Aside>

---

## v1.14.0

**Custom fields on connections — admin-define, collect, display**

Connection types can now carry their own data fields, wired end-to-end through the UI. The data model and API already supported `structure_connection_type.fields` and stored values on the connection node's `data` column — this release makes that capability usable from the app.

- **Admin** — the connection-type form (**Admin → Connection Types**) has a new **Fields** section: add fields with a label, a type (`text`, `textarea`, `number`, `select`, `multiselect`, `toggle`, `date`), an optional comma-separated options list (for `select`/`multiselect`), and a Required toggle.
- **Collect** — the **Add Connection** dialog now chooses the connection type from a `<Select>` of defined types and renders that type's fields as inputs. Required fields gate the submit, defaults are seeded, and the values are stored on the connection's `data` keyed by field id. Arbitrary/legacy types (e.g. `related`) remain creatable when no types are defined.
- **Display** — the connections list shows an info popover on each connection badge listing that connection's field values, resolving option labels for `select`/`multiselect` and `Yes`/`No` for toggles.

<Aside type="note">
These are changes to template strings, so the definitive check is `npx structure update` + a build in a starter app.
</Aside>

---

## v1.13.3

**Health-audit hardening (atomicity, validation, performance, UX)**

A four-dimension health audit (standards / production / performance / security) found **0 critical and 0 security-moderate** issues — the `userId`-scoping and `requireAdmin` controls are sound — and applied 23 fixes across the templates:

- **Backend:** `deleteNode` is now atomic (`prisma.$transaction`) and also soft-deletes connections pointing at descendants; `userId` scoping is threaded through `calculatePath`/`updateDescendantPaths` and every caller; the `dataFilters` query param is validated against `dataFilterSchema` (a `400` instead of a `500` on bad input) with length/array bounds.
- **Frontend data:** `listNodes` now sends the previously-dropped `pathPrefix`/`dataFilters`/`workflow*` params (the data-filter & workflow-state filtering was silently unreachable from the hooks); connection mutations invalidate `node-context`; `nodeTypeRegistry.update()`/`unregister()` notify `onSync` subscribers so admin edits refresh the nav reactively.
- **Frontend components:** the computed-template sync in `NodeFieldRenderer` moved out of render (no more update-during-render); `NodeSelector` resolves the selected node by id and debounces its search; `NodeCard`/`NodeListItem` are memoised with stable callbacks.
- **Frontend pages:** distinct error/retry states on the list, detail, children, and connections views; `NodeInfoTab` flushes pending edits on unmount (no lost edits); settings saves surface errors; admin `NodeTypeForm` and `NodeSettingsTab` validate required fields client-side.
- **Schema/standards:** composite `@@index([userId, path])`; removed an unused `tooltip` shadcn dependency; fixed a stale `initStructure()` comment; narrowed an `as never` cast.

Two minor performance refinements (a `getTree` column `select` and raw data-filter-query pagination) were **deferred** to avoid type/correctness regressions — see the roadmap.

<Aside type="note">
These are changes to template strings, so the definitive check is `npx structure update` + a build in a starter app.
</Aside>

---

## v1.13.2

**Connection-type `bidirectional` default fixed to `true`**

`createConnectionType` used `bidirectional: data.bidirectional ?? false`, which always sent an explicit value and overrode the schema's `@default(true)` (and disagreed with the admin create form, which defaults the toggle on). Changed the fallback to `?? true` so the Prisma default, the admin form, and the docs all agree the default is `true`.

## v1.13.1

**Cleanup + scaffold v1.12.1**

Housekeeping release. Removed the dead `my-feature` scaffold reference templates under `src/templates/frontend/` (`routes.ts`, `services.ts`, `hooks.ts`, `types.ts`) — the frontend counterpart to the backend cleanup in v1.13.0. Structure's real templates live inline in the per-area `index.ts` files, so these were unimported placeholder files; build and type-check confirm nothing referenced them. This fully resolves the long-standing "reconcile scaffold reference templates" roadmap item.

Updated the scaffold infrastructure to **v1.12.1**, which fixes three `scaffold:update` rough edges surfaced while integrating the security feature into this module: the module-owned restore is no longer skipped when a manual conflict (e.g. `CLAUDE.md`) interrupts the merge; `CLAUDE.md` now smart-merges instead of conflicting on every update; and `scaffold:check` no longer pushes a self-update during a read-only preview.

---

## v1.13.0

**Security feature integration — account-deletion cascade**

Structure now declares its security surface to the starter template's `securityRegistry` (scaffold v1.11.0+), so a user's Structure data is fully erased when their account is deleted — no orphaned rows.

A new generated `security.registration.ts` (a side-effect import in the feature's `index.ts`, alongside the route and service registrations) registers:

- A **manifest** declaring all six per-user data stores — `structure_nodes`, `structure_node_types`, `structure_node_type_groups`, `structure_templates`, `structure_connection_types`, `structure_settings` — as `owned` (hard-deleted on account erasure). Connections are stored as nodes, so they are covered by `structure_nodes`.
- A **deletion handler per store**, each idempotent (`deleteMany`) and scoped to the subject (`ctx.subject.userId`). For the nullable-`userId` configuration tables, only the user's own rows are removed; system and module defaults (`userId = null`) are preserved.

`ownedModels` is empty because the schema is `userId`-keyed — adopting `tenantId` + Neon RLS remains an opt-in upgrade. No field encryption or hashed secrets are declared: Structure stores only user-authored organisational data, with no credentials or sensitive columns.

**Cleanup:** removed three dead `my-feature` placeholder backend template files (`routes.ts`, `services.ts`, `types.ts`) and the duplicate, unused route/service registration exports left over from an earlier partial scaffold split.

<Aside type="note">
Requires the starter template's security feature (scaffold v1.11.0+). A frontend-only module — or a backend module that stores no per-user data — would instead delete `security.registration.ts` and its `index.ts` import.
</Aside>

---

## v1.10.0 — v1.12.2

**Undocumented interim releases**

Forty-eight releases (v1.10.0, v1.10.1, v1.11.0–v1.11.42, v1.12.0–v1.12.2) shipped without changelog entries during a period of rapid iteration. Their content is recoverable from the git history — there are no release tags, so walk the release markers with `git log --grep 'chore(release)'` and read the commits sitting between them. Nothing in that range is load-bearing for a current install — v1.13.0 onwards is the documented lineage.

A handful of later releases are likewise undocumented, and — unlike the block above — several of them carry real template changes that appear nowhere on this page: **v1.15.2** (register with the host module registry, starter v1.48+), **v1.15.3** (standards-alignment batches B–D), **v1.16.1** (EOL-normalise migration checksums), **v1.18.1** (§8 composition, §7 opacity and schema index alignment) and **v1.31.1** (`structure_field_template` added to `schema.modelNames`). Don't assume the next entry covers them — read the commits.

## v1.9.8

**Revert v1.9.7 `NodeTypeForm` Tabs rewrite**

v1.9.7 replaced shadcn `<Tabs>` with a hand-rolled state-driven tab bar in `NodeTypeForm` to work around a user-reported rendering issue (six tall columns beside a cramped sidebar). On reflection that was a hack — the module shouldn't avoid shadcn primitives just because one consuming app has a broken `@/components/ui/tabs`. The real bug lives in the host app's tabs component or its surrounding layout, and hiding it in a module template makes it harder to find the next time someone customises the form.

**Reverted:** `NodeTypeForm` is back to using `<Tabs>` / `<TabsList>` / `<TabsTrigger>` / `<TabsContent>` from `@/components/ui/tabs`. Root cause investigation is now a Known Issue on the roadmap.

---

## v1.9.6

**Retry the auto-prime — critical for non-admin users**

v1.9.5 restored the auto-prime fetch but with no retry. If that first fetch fails (cold-login race with JWT landing in storage, transient network error, backend startup lag), the registry stays empty. **Admins can click into the Admin area to force a `useNodeTypes()` fire**, which re-syncs the registry. **Non-admin users can't** — if their sidebar has no Structure items, they have no URL to navigate to, so they're stuck on whatever landing page the app dropped them on.

**Fix:** the auto-prime now retries on failure with backoff — attempt 0 immediately, then +1s, +2s, +3s. Total ~6s window. After that it gives up silently, but by then auth has almost always initialised. Every subsequent retry uses the shared JWT-aware axios client, so it picks up the token as soon as it lands.

```ts
function primeStructureNavigation(attempt = 0): void {
  structureService.listNodeTypes()
    .then((types) => nodeTypeRegistry.syncFromDatabase(types))
    .catch(() => {
      if (attempt < 3) {
        setTimeout(() => primeStructureNavigation(attempt + 1), (attempt + 1) * 1000);
      }
    });
}
```

`<StructureInit />` and `initStructureNavigation()` are still available as explicit opt-in fallbacks, but apps shouldn't need them in the typical auth flow.

---

## v1.9.5

**Restore auto-priming navigation — revert v1.9.2 over-correction**

The v1.9.2 release removed the module-scope `structureService.listNodeTypes()` call, citing a pre-auth 401 on fresh page loads. That turned out to be an over-correction — the 401 was cosmetic (silent-caught), but removing the fetch broke nav population for any user who didn't visit a Structure page. v1.9.4's `<StructureInit />` component fixed it *if* apps wired it in, but users reported the sidebar still looked empty — the component only helps when consuming apps mount it, and the expectation is "it should just work like it used to".

**Fix:** the auto-prime fetch at the bottom of `NAVIGATION_REGISTRATION` is back. Same behaviour as pre-v1.9.2 — fires once on module import, uses the shared JWT-aware axios client, silent-catches errors so logged-out cold loads don't surface noise. The feature's `index.ts` is imported by the authenticated app shell, so the fetch runs post-login in practice.

```ts
structureService.listNodeTypes()
  .then((types) => nodeTypeRegistry.syncFromDatabase(types))
  .catch(() => {
    // Silent fail — a later useNodeTypes() will populate the registry
  });
```

**`<StructureInit />` is kept** as an optional helper. Use cases documented in its docstring — mainly apps whose auth flow means the feature module loads before the JWT is available. Post-Installation steps no longer list it as required; the sidebar just works on first render.

**Apps upgrading:** `npx structure update` pulls the revert; no app-level wiring needed. If you added `<StructureInit />` in v1.9.4, it still works and is harmless — just optional now.

---

## v1.9.4

**`<StructureInit />` — fix cold-load empty sidebar**

Fixes a user-reported bug introduced in v1.9.2: the main sidebar's Structure nav items (node types with `mainNav.enabled`) were missing on first load post-login, and only appeared after the user visited a Structure page (typically the admin page). Non-admin users who never had a reason to open admin would see an empty sidebar indefinitely.

**Root cause:** v1.9.2 removed the module-import-time `structureService.listNodeTypes()` call (it was hitting the API pre-auth and 401'ing on every fresh page load). The intended replacement was the opt-in `initStructureNavigation()` helper, documented as "call from a post-auth init point". That was too easy to overlook.

**Fix:** New `<StructureInit />` component — renders nothing, calls `useNodeTypes()` internally. Apps mount it once in their authenticated layout, and the sidebar populates on first render instead of waiting for the user to visit a Structure page.

```tsx
// frontend/src/layouts/AuthenticatedLayout.tsx
import { StructureInit } from '@/features/structure';

export function AuthenticatedLayout({ children }) {
  return (
    <>
      <StructureInit />
      {children}
    </>
  );
}
```

Existing `initStructureNavigation()` imperative helper kept for apps that need to prime the registry outside React (e.g. from a non-component init script).

**Post-install docs updated** to make mounting `<StructureInit />` a numbered required step, with the gotcha called out that it must live below the auth boundary (it calls the API on mount).

---

**Documentation accuracy audit — all findings resolved**

Comprehensive pass over `configuration.mdx`, `usage.mdx`, `integration.mdx`, and `index.mdx` to bring the docs back in sync with the code.

**Interface drift fixed (configuration.mdx):**
- `NodeTypeFeatures` block rewritten — real fields are `slug`, `description`, `color`, `icon`, `favorites`, `archiving`, `publishing`, `manualSort` (not the `can*`-prefixed versions the docs previously listed)
- `NodeTypeRules` block rewritten with the real fields: `isRoot`, `allowedParents`, `allowedChildren`, `maxDepth`, `maxChildren`, `isHierarchical`, `allowMultipleParents`, `isConnection`, `connectionConfig`
- `NodeTypeConfig` optionality matches code — `fields`, `icon`, `isSystem`, `sortOrder`, `moduleId` are all optional; `moduleId: string | null`
- `NodeTypeNavigation.sidebarNav.style` adds `'none'` option and missing sub-fields (`parentType`, `maxDepth`, `showEmpty`, `collapsible`, `defaultExpanded`)

- `usage.mdx` `NodeSelector` example — uses `allowedTypes` prop; `onChange` receives an ID string, not a node object (matches the actual component)
- `integration.mdx` dependency declaration — `requiredModules: ['structure']` (not the non-existent top-level `dependencies` field)
- `integration.mdx` node type init example — uses a real `SYSTEM_USER_ID` constant instead of the literal `'system'` string that would fail the FK check

**Outdated hook signatures fixed (usage.mdx):**
- `useNodeTree(rootId)` → `useNodeTree(nodeType?: string)`
- `useNodeMutations` description no longer claims optimistic updates (it uses cache invalidation)
- Hook tables reorganised into Node Data / Node Type Data / Mutations / Registry / Init Helper

- `useNodeConnections` moved from "Navigation Hooks" to the data hooks

**NodeTypeRegistry API clarified (integration.mdx):**
- Full method table distinguishing methods that return registration wrappers (`get`, `getAll`) from methods that return bare configs (`getConfig`, `getAllTypes`, `getEnabledTypes`, `getByModule`)
- Added `register`, `unregister`, `update`, `has`, `isLoadedFromDb`, `getSlugs` which weren't documented

**Registry count unified:**
- All four pages now consistently say "9 module-specific registries — 8 content registries shown in the Registries admin tab, plus `nodeTypeRegistry` managed via Structure Types"

**Enhancements:**
- Complete CLI reference table added to `configuration.mdx` and `index.mdx` covering `migrate`, `disable`, `enable`, and all missing flags (`--no-interactive`, `--dry-run`, `--force`, `--keep-data`, `--export`)
- Post-Installation gotchas added to `configuration.mdx` (`hasInit` requirement, route ordering, system-level seeding)
- Import snippets added above each of the 8 registry reference tables in `integration.mdx`
- Settings page note clarifies it uses TanStack Query with debounced live-save

**Template change:**

## v1.9.2

**Standards alignment follow-up — audit findings resolved**

Cleanup pass on findings from the v1.9.1 audit.

**`console.*` removed from generated code:**
- `pages.ts` (NodeConnectionsTab + NodeTypesAdminPage) — 5 `console.error` in catch blocks replaced with `toast.error(err.message)`
- `registrations.ts` (NAVIGATION_REGISTRATION) — `console.debug` removed along with its containing module-import fetch (see below)

**Module-import API call removed:**
- `NAVIGATION_REGISTRATION` no longer fires `structureService.listNodeTypes()` at module load — that call was running pre-auth on every fresh page load and 401ing silently
- Replaced with an optional `initStructureNavigation()` function for apps that want to prime the nav before any page mounts. In most cases no call is needed — `useNodeTypes()` on any Structure page syncs the registry and triggers the navigation listener automatically

**TanStack Query migration extended to admin + connection CRUD:**
- New `useNodeTypeMutations()` hook (in `useNodeTypes.ts`): `createNodeType`, `updateNodeType`, `deleteNodeType`, `reorderNodeTypes` — all fire `toast.success`/`toast.error`, invalidate `['structure', 'node-types']` on success, and mirror the result back into `nodeTypeRegistry`
- New `useConnectionMutations()` hook (in `useNode.ts`): `createConnection`, `deleteConnection` — invalidate `['structure', 'node-connections']` and `['structure', 'nodes']` (for list connection counts)
- `NodeConnectionsTab` and `NodeTypesAdminPage` rewired to consume these hooks; direct `structureService.*` calls and manual `await refetch()` are gone
- Reorder uses a single batch mutation (N parallel updates + one invalidation) instead of firing N toasts and N refetches

**Polish:**
- `useNodeMutations.updateNode` now toasts `'Node updated'` on success (previously silent — inconsistent with create/delete/move/etc.)
- `reorderNodes` in backend `SERVICE_TEMPLATE` now logs `log.info({ userId, parentId, count }, 'Nodes reordered')` — matches the logging pattern of every other mutation
- `mediaType` string-literal union renamed from `'any'` to `'all'` across frontend type, backend type, and admin field-config UI; user-facing label stays "Any". Removes the read-as-TypeScript-keyword confusion

## v1.9.1

**Scaffold v1.9.0 + TanStack Query Migration**

Standards alignment release bringing the module in line with the current LLM rules.

**Scaffold Update (v1.5.0 → v1.9.0):**
- Pulled four cumulative scaffold releases: styleRegistry support (v1.6.0), template performance pass (v1.7.0), debounced live-save + deferred hooks (v1.8.0), TanStack Query hooks template + module-starter docs accuracy (v1.9.0)
- New template files: `src/templates/frontend/hooks.ts` (scaffold reference), `backend/registrations.ts`
- Extended `ModuleDefinition` schema with 37 additional type lines

**Data Fetching Migration:**
- All server-fetching hooks (`useNodes`, `useNode`, `useNodeMutations`, `useNodeTypes`, `useNodeTree`, `useTemplates`) now use `useQuery` / `useMutation` from `@tanstack/react-query`
- Module-scoped cache keys via `createModuleQueryKeys('structure')` prevent collisions with other modules sharing the QueryClient
- Mutations invalidate the relevant subkey prefixes (`nodes`, `node`, `node-tree`, etc.) on success — stale list/detail/tree views refetch automatically
- Registry-only hooks (`useNodeTabs`, `useNodeWidgets`, `useNodeActions`, `useNodeColumns`, `useNodeFilters`, `useNodeSidebarSections`, `useStructureNavigation`) remain unchanged — they subscribe to in-memory module registries, not server state
- `useNodeMutations` preserves its pre-migration aggregate return shape (`{ createNode, isCreating, error, ... }`) for consumer compatibility
- `useTemplates` still returns `templates` (not `data`) for backward compatibility with existing pages

**Consumer-Facing Change:**
- `data` is now `undefined` (not `null`) while queries are pending — consumers using `if (!data)` keep working; any `data === null` check needs updating

## v1.9.0

**Action & Filter Hook Firing, Structured Logging, Settings Page, Lifecycle Hooks**

Major standards alignment release. Action and filter hooks that were previously registered but never fired are now fully wired into the backend service.

**Action Hooks Now Fire:**

All 16 action hooks documented in `action.registration.ts` now fire after their corresponding service mutations via `void actionRegistry.doActionAll('hook.name', context)`.

New hooks added:
- `structure.node.reordered` — fires after batch reorder
- `structure.template.created` / `.updated` / `.deleted` — template lifecycle events
- `structure.settings.updated` — user settings changes

**Filter Hooks Now Applied:**

All 4 filter hooks are applied at the correct pipeline points:
- `structure.node.beforeCreate` — applied to input before node creation
- `structure.node.response` — applied to node data before returning from `getNode`
- `structure.node.listQuery` — applied to query parameters before building the Prisma where clause
- `structure.nodeType.response` — applied to each node type in `listNodeTypes` and `getNodeType`

New filter registration: `structure.nodeType.response` default pass-through filter added to `filter.registration.ts`.

**Structured Logging:**
- Backend service uses Pino structured logging via `createModuleLogger('structure')`
- All mutations log at info level with userId, nodeId/slug, and operation description
- Blocked operations (e.g., deleting a node type with existing nodes) log at warn level
- Read operations are not logged

**Settings Page:**
- User-facing settings page registered at `/settings/structure` via `settingsPagesRegistry`
- Preferences for default view, show favourites, sidebar state, item counts
- Uses `settings-blocks` components (Row, SectionHeader, Divider, Note, ToggleSwitch, Dropdown)

**Lifecycle Hooks:**
- `onInstall` hook logs success message and note about default type seeding
- `onUpdate` hook logs version transition, warns on major version changes, notes schema updates

**Scaffold Update:**
- Updated from scaffold v1.4.19 to v1.5.0

## v1.8.0

**Expanded Registry System**

Five new registries, customisable labels, and a Registries admin tab.

**New Registries:**
- `nodeActionRegistry` — Register action buttons and menu items for node detail headers, context menus, and bulk action bars. Supports confirmation dialogs, keyboard shortcuts, and bulk handlers.
- `nodeFieldTypeRegistry` — Register custom field types that extend the built-in field editor. Structure ships with 13 built-in types (text, textarea, number, date, datetime, select, multiselect, toggle, url, email, secret, node-reference, media) registered via `field-types.registration.ts`.
- `nodeColumnRegistry` — Register custom columns for the table view on list pages. Supports sortable columns, custom widths, alignment, compact renderers, and data prefetching.
- `nodeFilterRegistry` — Register filter controls for list pages. Supports client-side filtering (in-browser) and server-side filtering (API query params).
- `nodeSidebarSectionRegistry` — Register custom sections for the detail page sidebar. Supports collapsible sections with configurable default state.

**New Features:**
- `NodeTypeLabels` — Customisable UI strings per node type (addNew, editItem, deleteItem, archiveAction, searchItems, emptyState, allItems, etc.)
- `getLabel()` utility — Smart label resolution with three-tier fallback: custom label → auto-generated from type name → generic default
- Registries admin tab — Browse all registered items across 8 registry types (actions, columns, filters, sidebar sections, field types, tabs, widgets, card extensions) with per-module and per-node-type filtering
- `NodeFieldRenderer` now checks the field type registry for module-provided types before falling back to built-in rendering
- `StructureModuleRegistration` expanded with 5 new optional arrays for bulk registration

**New Hooks:**
- `useNodeActions(node, nodeType)` — Filtered actions for a node with admin overrides
- `useNodeColumns(nodeType)` — Columns for a node type's table view
- `useNodeFilters(nodeType)` — Filter controls with state management (`activeFilters`, `setFilter`, `clearFilters`)
- `useNodeSidebarSections(node, nodeType)` — Sidebar sections for a node

**Admin Improvements:**
- Third top-level tab "Registries" on the App Structure admin page
- Registry Explorer in left pane showing item counts per registry type
- Detailed table view of registered items showing id, module, label, and registry-specific fields

**Backend Changes:**
- Added `NodeTypeLabels` interface to backend types
- Added `labels` property to `NodeTypeConfig` and `createNodeTypeSchema`
- Expanded `NodeTypeIntegrations` with `actions`, `columns`, `filters`, `sidebarSections`

## v1.5.0

**Comprehensive Template Cleanup and Feature Additions**

Major overhaul of all templates: bug fixes, new admin features, reactivity fixes, and code cleanup.

**New Features:**
- Card builder with live preview in admin Pages tab (`CardPreview` component)
- Configurable list page filters - admins define which fields appear as filter dropdowns
- Column configuration for table view in list pages
- Detail page navigation styles: tabs, sidebar, or none (`pageNav.style`)

**Reactivity Fixes:**
- `NodeTypeRegistry.onSync()` now returns unsubscribe function (prevents memory leaks)
- `useEnabledNodeTypes` hook properly subscribes to onSync events and re-renders on changes
- `useStructureNavItems` hook properly subscribes to onSync events with cleanup
- `useNodes` hook uses `useMemo` for query decomposition instead of `JSON.stringify`
- `useNodeTree` uses shared `NodeTreeItem` type instead of local duplicate
- `useNodeConnections` hook supports `refetch` pattern

**Admin Improvements:**
- Explicit mode management (`list`, `create`, `edit`, `duplicate`) replaces fragile slug detection
- `NodeTypeForm` accepts `mode` prop instead of inferring from slug string
- Filterable fields configuration section in Pages tab
- Column configuration section in Pages tab

**Cleanup:**
- Removed `isEnabled` from frontend `NodeTypeConfig` interface (kept in database for future use)
- Removed unused `Filter` import from NodeListPage (re-added properly with filter implementation)
- Fixed invalid Lucide icons: `Lightning` → `Bolt`, `Magic` → `Sparkle`
- Removed unused `parentId` prop from `CreateNodeButton`
- Added `scroll-area` to shadcn/ui components
- Renamed `NodeHeader` → `NodeDetailHeader`
- Added schema comment explaining self-referential `@relation` directives

**Type Additions:**
- `card`, `columns`, `filterableFields`, `defaultSort` added to `listPage` navigation config (frontend and backend)

## Versioning

Version bumps happen via the module's version scripts, following the module-starter's [patch-default cadence](/module-starter/versioning/#version-bump-commands):

```bash
npm run version:patch  # The default — fixes, new templates, new features, additive schema
npm run version:minor  # Rare — a genuinely new subsystem, or a headline release
npm run version:major  # A deliberate breaking epoch, post-launch
```

Reaching for `minor` because a change "adds a feature" is almost always wrong — on this module that is a patch.

Any change to files in `src/templates/` or `src/module.ts` requires a version bump so that apps know updates are available.
